  Sam: I think Quinn's calmness could be a red flag, but I also noticed that Sam didn't speak up much during the night phase. If we're trying to figure out who might be a werewolf, we should consider who had the chance to act without being noticed. Sam's silence might be suspicious. Let's keep an open mind and not jump to conclusions.
  Quinn: I think we need to look at both actions and reactions. While Quinn's calmness is notable, Sam's silence during the night could also be a sign of hiding something. However, we should also consider who had the most opportunity to act. Since David was killed, maybe someone who was close to him or had a reason to target him. Let's keep an open discussion and not assume too quickly.
  Kate: I think we should focus on who had the opportunity to act during the night. While Quinn's calmness and Sam's silence are worth noting, I didn’t see any direct evidence pointing to them. I’m more concerned about Jack and Leo—they were very quiet during the night and didn’t seem to react to David’s death. That kind of behavior could be suspicious. Let’s keep an eye on them.
  Grace: I investigated Quinn last night and found them to be a Villager. While their calmness may raise questions, there's no evidence they are a werewolf. I think we should focus on others who haven’t been ruled out, like Jack and Leo, as they were unusually quiet during the night. Let’s keep discussing and use logic to narrow down suspects.
  Yara: Grace, thank you for sharing your investigation—Quinn is safe. That changes things. I think we should focus on Jack and Leo now, as Kate mentioned, since they were unusually quiet and didn’t react to David’s death. Their behavior is suspicious, and we need to keep them in mind for the vote. Let’s keep discussing and make sure we don’t overlook anyone.

=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (11): Noah, Sam, Quinn, Kate, Grace, Yara, Wendy, Bob, Paul, Jack, Leo

  Day 1: David (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Noah: I think we need to focus on who might be the werewolf based on their behavior last night. David was killed, and we should consider who had the most opportunity or suspicious actions. I’m leaning towards Quinn because they seemed too calm and didn’t show much concern during the night phase. Let’s discuss and vote carefully.
  Sam: I think Quinn's calmness could be a red flag, but I also noticed that Sam didn't speak up much during the night phase. If we're trying to figure out who might be a werewolf, we should consider who had the chance to act without being noticed. Sam's silence might be suspicious. Let's keep an open mind and not jump to conclusions.
  Quinn: I think we need to look at both actions and reactions. While Quinn's calmness is notable, Sam's silence during the night could also be a sign of hiding something. However, we should also consider who had the most opportunity to act. Since David was killed, maybe someone who was close to him or had a reason to target him. Let's keep an open discussion and not assume too quickly.
  Kate: I think we should focus on who had the opportunity to act during the night. While Quinn's calmness and Sam's silence are worth noting, I didn’t see any direct evidence pointing to them. I’m more concerned about Jack and Leo—they were very quiet during the night and didn’t seem to react to David’s death. That kind of behavior could be suspicious. Let’s keep an eye on them.
  Grace: I investigated Quinn last night and found them to be a Villager. While their calmness may raise questions, there's no evidence they are a werewolf. I think we should focus on others who haven’t been ruled out, like Jack and Leo, as they were unusually quiet during the night. Let’s keep discussing and use logic to narrow down suspects.
  Yara: Grace, thank you for sharing your investigation—Quinn is safe. That changes things. I think we should focus on Jack and Leo now, as Kate mentioned, since they were unusually quiet and didn’t react to David’s death. Their behavior is suspicious, and we need to keep them in mind for the vote. Let’s keep discussing and make sure we don’t overlook anyone.
  Wendy: Thank you, Grace, for confirming Quinn is safe. That helps narrow things down. I agree with Kate and Yara—Jack and Leo’s silence during the night is concerning. They didn’t react to David’s death, which is unusual. I think we should focus our suspicion on them and consider voting them out next. Let’s keep the discussion open but stay logical.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.
Respond with: STATEMENT: <your message>

[COMPLETION]
STATEMENT: I agree with the shift in focus to Jack and Leo— their silence during the night and lack of reaction to David’s death are definitely suspicious. However, we should also consider that the werewolf might be trying to avoid drawing attention by staying quiet. I think we need to weigh their behavior against others and make sure we’re not overlooking someone else. Let’s keep the discussion open and vote based on the most consistent signs of suspicion.

  Sam: I think Quinn's calmness could be a red flag, but I also noticed that Sam didn't speak up much during the night phase. Let's keep an open mind and not jump to conclusions.
  Quinn: I think we need to look at both actions and reactions. While Quinn's calmness is notable, Sam's silence during the night could also be a sign of hiding something.
